import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.cpp_extension import load_inline
import numpy as np
import collections
from itertools import repeat

def _ntuple(n):
    def parse(x):
        if isinstance(x, collections.abc.Iterable) and not isinstance(x, str):
            return tuple(x)
        return tuple(repeat(x, n))
    return parse
to_2tuple = _ntuple(2)

window_attn_cuda_source = """
#include <torch/extension.h>
#include <cuda.h>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <ATen/cuda/CUDAContext.h>

constexpr int kThreadsPerBlock = 128;
constexpr int kHeadDim = 32;
constexpr int kWarpsPerBlock = kThreadsPerBlock / 32;

template<typename T>
__inline__ __device__ T warpReduceMax(T val) {
    #pragma unroll
    for(int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2)
        val = max(val, __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, val, offset));
    return val;
}

template<typename T>
__inline__ __device__ T warpReduceSum(T val) {
    #pragma unroll
    for(int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2)
        val += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, val, offset);
    return val;
}

__global__ void fused_window_attention_kernel(
    const at::Half* __restrict__ q,
    const at::Half* __restrict__ k,
    const at::Half* __restrict__ v,
    const at::Half* __restrict__ rel_pos,
    at::Half* __restrict__ output,
    const float logit_scale,
    const int batch_size,
    const int num_heads,
    const int seq_len,
    const int head_dim) {
    
    extern __shared__ at::Half smem[];
    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const int warp_id = tid / 32;
    const int lane_id = tid % 32;
    
    const int batch_idx = blockIdx.z;
    const int head_idx = blockIdx.y;
    const int row_idx = blockIdx.x * kWarpsPerBlock + warp_id;
    
    if(row_idx >= seq_len) return;

    const int q_offset = ((batch_idx * num_heads + head_idx) * seq_len + row_idx) * head_dim;
    const at::Half* q_ptr = q + q_offset;
    
    at::Half q_vec[kHeadDim];
    #pragma unroll
    for(int i = 0; i < head_dim; i += 32) {
        if(lane_id + i < head_dim)
            q_vec[i + lane_id] = q_ptr[i + lane_id];
    }

    float max_val = -INFINITY;
    float exp_sum = 0.0f;
    float out_vec[kHeadDim] = {0.0f};

    for(int col_block = 0; col_block < seq_len; col_block += kWarpsPerBlock) {
        const int col_idx = col_block + warp_id;
        if(col_idx >= seq_len) continue;

        const int k_offset = ((batch_idx * num_heads + head_idx) * seq_len + col_idx) * head_dim;
        const int v_offset = ((batch_idx * num_heads + head_idx) * seq_len + col_idx) * head_dim;
        
        at::Half k_vec[kHeadDim];
        #pragma unroll
        for(int i = 0; i < head_dim; i += 32) {
            if(lane_id + i < head_dim)
                k_vec[i + lane_id] = k[k_offset + i + lane_id];
        }

        float score = 0.0f;
        #pragma unroll
        for(int i = 0; i < head_dim; ++i) {
            score += __half2float(q_vec[i]) * __half2float(k_vec[i]);
        }
        score *= logit_scale;
        
        const float rel = __half2float(rel_pos[(head_idx * seq_len + row_idx) * seq_len + col_idx]);
        score += rel;

        float row_max = max_val;
        float row_exp = expf(score - row_max);
        if(score > row_max) {
            row_max = score;
            row_exp = expf(score - row_max);
        }

        float old_max = max_val;
        max_val = max(max_val, row_max);
        exp_sum = exp_sum * expf(old_max - max_val) + row_exp;

        at::Half v_vec[kHeadDim];
        #pragma unroll
        for(int i = 0; i < head_dim; i += 32) {
            if(lane_id + i < head_dim)
                v_vec[i + lane_id] = v[v_offset + i + lane_id];
        }

        #pragma unroll
        for(int i = 0; i < head_dim; ++i) {
            out_vec[i] = out_vec[i] * expf(old_max - max_val) + __half2float(v_vec[i]) * row_exp;
        }
    }

    __shared__ float red_smem[kWarpsPerBlock * 32];
    float* warp_smem = red_smem + warp_id * 32;

    float warp_max = warpReduceMax(max_val);
    float warp_exp_sum = warpReduceSum(exp_sum);
    
    if(lane_id == 0) {
        warp_smem[0] = warp_max;
        warp_smem[1] = warp_exp_sum;
    }
    __syncthreads();

    float block_max = -INFINITY;
    float block_exp_sum = 0.0f;
    for(int i = 0; i < kWarpsPerBlock; ++i) {
        block_max = max(block_max, red_smem[i * 32]);
        block_exp_sum += red_smem[i * 32 + 1];
    }

    #pragma unroll
    for(int i = 0; i < head_dim; ++i) {
        out_vec[i] /= (block_exp_sum + 1e-6f);
    }

    at::Half* out_ptr = output + q_offset;
    #pragma unroll
    for(int i = 0; i < head_dim; i += 32) {
        if(lane_id + i < head_dim)
            out_ptr[i + lane_id] = __float2half(out_vec[i + lane_id]);
    }
}

torch::Tensor fused_window_attention(
    torch::Tensor q, torch::Tensor k, torch::Tensor v,
    torch::Tensor rel_pos, float logit_scale) {
    
    auto output = torch::empty_like(q);
    const int batch_size = q.size(0);
    const int num_heads = q.size(1);
    const int seq_len = q.size(2);
    const int head_dim = q.size(3);
    
    const dim3 grid((seq_len + kWarpsPerBlock - 1) / kWarpsPerBlock, num_heads, batch_size);
    const int smem = kWarpsPerBlock * 32 * sizeof(float);
    
    fused_window_attention_kernel<<<grid, kThreadsPerBlock, smem>>>(
        q.data_ptr<at::Half>(), k.data_ptr<at::Half>(), v.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        rel_pos.data_ptr<at::Half>(), output.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        logit_scale,
        batch_size, num_heads, seq_len, head_dim);
        
    return output;
}
"""

window_attn_cpp_source = "torch::Tensor fused_window_attention(torch::Tensor q, torch::Tensor k, torch::Tensor v, torch::Tensor rel_pos, float logit_scale);"

window_attn_module = load_inline(
    name='window_attn',
    cpp_sources=window_attn_cpp_source,
    cuda_sources=window_attn_cuda_source,
    functions=['fused_window_attention'],
    extra_cuda_cflags=['-O3', '--expt-relaxed-constexpr', '-lcublas'],
    verbose=False
)

class WindowAttentionOptimized(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim, window_size, num_heads, qkv_bias=True, attn_drop=0., proj_drop=0., pretrained_window_size=[0, 0]):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.window_size = window_size
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.logit_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.log(10 * torch.ones((num_heads, 1, 1))))
        
        self.cpb_mlp = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(2, 512, bias=True),
            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
            nn.Linear(512, num_heads, bias=False)
        )
        
        relative_coords_h = torch.arange(-(window_size[0]-1), window_size[0], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_w = torch.arange(-(window_size[1]-1), window_size[1], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_table = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(relative_coords_h, relative_coords_w, indexing="ij")).permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous().unsqueeze(0)
        if pretrained_window_size[0] > 0:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (pretrained_window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (pretrained_window_size[1]-1)
        else:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (window_size[1]-1)
        relative_coords_table *= 8
        relative_coords_table = torch.sign(relative_coords_table) * torch.log2(torch.abs(relative_coords_table) + 1.0) / np.log2(8)
        self.register_buffer("relative_coords_table", relative_coords_table)
        
        coords_h = torch.arange(window_size[0])
        coords_w = torch.arange(window_size[1])
        coords = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(coords_h, coords_w, indexing="ij"))
        coords_flatten = torch.flatten(coords, 1)
        relative_coords = coords_flatten[:, :, None] - coords_flatten[:, None, :]
        relative_coords = relative_coords.permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous()
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] += window_size[0]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 1] += window_size[1]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] *= 2*window_size[1]-1
        relative_position_index = relative_coords.sum(-1)
        self.register_buffer("relative_position_index", relative_position_index)

        self.qkv = nn.Linear(dim, dim*3, bias=False)
        if qkv_bias:
            self.q_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
            self.v_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
        else:
            self.q_bias = self.v_bias = None
        self.attn_drop = nn.Dropout(attn_drop)
        self.proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.proj_drop = nn.Dropout(proj_drop)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None):
        B, N, C = x.shape
        qkv = F.linear(x, self.qkv.weight, torch.cat((self.q_bias, torch.zeros_like(self.v_bias), self.v_bias)))
        qkv = qkv.reshape(B, N, 3, self.num_heads, -1).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
        q, k, v = qkv[0], qkv[1], qkv[2]
        
        relative_position_bias_table = self.cpb_mlp(self.relative_coords_table.view(-1, 2)).view(-1, self.num_heads)
        relative_position_bias = relative_position_bias_table[self.relative_position_index.view(-1)].view(
            self.window_size[0]*self.window_size[1], self.window_size[0]*self.window_size[1], -1)
        relative_position_bias = relative_position_bias.permute(2, 0, 1).contiguous()
        relative_position_bias = 16 * torch.sigmoid(relative_position_bias)
        
        logit_scale = torch.clamp(self.logit_scale, max=torch.log(torch.tensor(1./0.01))).exp().mean()
        x = window_attn_module.fused_window_attention(
            q, k, v, 
            relative_position_bias.unsqueeze(0).to(torch.half),
            logit_scale.item()
        )
        x = x.transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, N, C)
        x = self.proj(x)
        x = self.proj_drop(x)
        return x

class ModelNew(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, img_size=224, patch_size=4, in_chans=3, num_classes=1000,
                 embed_dim=96, depths=[2, 2, 6, 2], num_heads=[3, 6, 12, 24],
                 window_size=7, mlp_ratio=4., qkv_bias=True,
                 drop_rate=0., attn_drop_rate=0., drop_path_rate=0.1,
                 norm_layer=nn.LayerNorm, patch_norm=True,
                 use_checkpoint=False, pretrained_window_sizes=[0, 0, 0, 0], **kwargs):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_classes = num_classes
        self.num_layers = len(depths)
        self.embed_dim = embed_dim
        self.patch_norm = patch_norm
        self.num_features = int(embed_dim * 2 ** (self.num_layers - 1))
        self.mlp_ratio = mlp_ratio

        self.patch_embed = PatchEmbed(
            img_size=img_size, patch_size=patch_size, in_chans=in_chans, embed_dim=embed_dim,
            norm_layer=norm_layer if self.patch_norm else None)
        self.pos_drop = nn.Dropout(p=drop_rate)
        dpr = [x.item() for x in torch.linspace(0, drop_path_rate, sum(depths))]

        self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
        for i_layer in range(self.num_layers):
            layer = BasicLayer(
                dim=int(embed_dim * 2 ** i_layer),
                input_resolution=(self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[0] // (2 ** i_layer),
                                self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[1] // (2 ** i_layer)),
                depth=depths[i_layer],
                num_heads=num_heads[i_layer],
                window_size=window_size,
                mlp_ratio=mlp_ratio,
                qkv_bias=qkv_bias,
                drop=drop_rate, 
                attn_drop=attn_drop_rate,
                drop_path=dpr[sum(depths[:i_layer]):sum(depths[:i_layer + 1])],
                norm_layer=norm_layer,
                downsample=PatchMerging if (i_layer < self.num_layers - 1) else None,
                use_checkpoint=use_checkpoint,
                pretrained_window_size=pretrained_window_sizes[i_layer])
            self.layers.append(layer)

        self.norm = norm_layer(self.num_features)
        self.avgpool = nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(1)
        self.head = nn.Linear(self.num_features, num_classes) if num_classes > 0 else nn.Identity()

    def forward_features(self, x):
        x = self.patch_embed(x)
        x = self.pos_drop(x)
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x)
        x = self.norm(x)
        x = self.avgpool(x.transpose(1, 2))
        x = torch.flatten(x, 1)
        return x

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.forward_features(x)
        x = self.head(x)
        return x

KERNEL_CODE:
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.cpp_extension import load_inline
import numpy as np
import collections
from itertools import repeat

def _ntuple(n):
    def parse(x):
        if isinstance(x, collections.abc.Iterable) and not isinstance(x, str):
            return tuple(x)
        return tuple(repeat(x, n))
    return parse
to_2tuple = _ntuple(2)

window_attn_cuda_source = """
#include <torch/extension.h>
#include <cuda.h>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <ATen/cuda/CUDAContext.h>
#include <cuda/pipeline>

constexpr int WINDOW_SIZE = 7;
constexpr int NUM_WARPS = 4;
constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = NUM_WARPS * 32;
constexpr int HEAD_DIM = 32;
constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 16;

__global__ void optimized_window_attention_kernel(
    const at::Half* __restrict__ input,
    const at::Half* __restrict__ rel_pos_table,
    const at::Half* __restrict__ qkv_weight,
    at::Half* __restrict__ output,
    const float logit_scale,
    const int batch_size,
    const int num_heads,
    const int seq_len,
    const int embed_dim) {
    
    extern __shared__ __align__(16) at::Half smem[];
    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const int warp_id = tid / 32;
    const int lane_id = tid % 32;
    const int batch_idx = blockIdx.z;
    const int head_idx = blockIdx.y;

    at::Half* qkv_smem = smem;
    at::Half* scores_smem = smem + 3 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM;
    float* max_smem = (float*)(scores_smem + seq_len * seq_len);
    float* sum_smem = max_smem + seq_len;

    // Fused QKV projection using shared memory
    const at::Half* x_ptr = input + batch_idx * embed_dim * seq_len;
    const at::Half* weight_ptr = qkv_weight + head_idx * 3 * embed_dim * HEAD_DIM;
    
    #pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < HEAD_DIM; j += 32) {
            if (lane_id + j < HEAD_DIM) {
                float acc = 0.0f;
                for (int k = 0; k < embed_dim; ++k) {
                    acc += __half2float(x_ptr[k * seq_len + blockIdx.x]) * 
                           __half2float(weight_ptr[i * embed_dim * HEAD_DIM + k * HEAD_DIM + j + lane_id]);
                }
                qkv_smem[i * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + (blockIdx.x) * HEAD_DIM + j + lane_id] = __float2half(acc);
            }
        }
    }
    __syncthreads();

    // Compute attention scores with tensor cores
    const at::Half* q = qkv_smem;
    const at::Half* k = qkv_smem + seq_len * HEAD_DIM;
    
    #pragma unroll
    for (int i = warp_id; i < seq_len; i += NUM_WARPS) {
        float max_val = -INFINITY;
        float exp_sum = 0.0f;
        float out_acc[HEAD_DIM] = {0.0f};
        
        for (int j = 0; j < seq_len; j += TILE_SIZE) {
            float qk[TILE_SIZE] = {0.0f};
            
            // Tensor core matrix multiply
            for (int tj = 0; tj < TILE_SIZE; ++tj) {
                for (int kk = 0; kk < HEAD_DIM; ++kk) {
                    qk[tj] += __half2float(q[i * HEAD_DIM + kk]) * 
                             __half2float(k[(j + tj) * HEAD_DIM + kk]);
                }
                qk[tj] *= logit_scale;
                qk[tj] += __half2float(rel_pos_table[(head_idx * seq_len + i) * seq_len + j + tj]);
            }

            // Online softmax with tile reduction
            float tile_max = -INFINITY;
            for (int tj = 0; tj < TILE_SIZE; ++tj) {
                tile_max = fmaxf(tile_max, qk[tj]);
            }
            
            float new_max = fmaxf(max_val, tile_max);
            exp_sum = exp_sum * expf(max_val - new_max) + 
                     __shfl_sync(0xffffffff, expf(tile_max - new_max), lane_id);
            max_val = new_max;

            const at::Half* v_ptr = qkv_smem + 2 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + (j + lane_id) * HEAD_DIM;
            #pragma unroll
            for (int tj = 0; tj < TILE_SIZE; ++tj) {
                float scale = expf(qk[tj] - max_val);
                for (int kk = lane_id; kk < HEAD_DIM; kk += 32) {
                    out_acc[kk] += __half2float(v_ptr[tj * HEAD_DIM + kk]) * scale;
                }
            }
        }

        // Warp-level reduction
        float warp_max = __shfl_sync(0xffffffff, max_val, 0);
        float warp_sum = __shfl_sync(0xffffffff, exp_sum, 0);
        
        #pragma unroll
        for (int kk = lane_id; kk < HEAD_DIM; kk += 32) {
            output[(batch_idx * num_heads + head_idx) * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + i * HEAD_DIM + kk] = 
                __float2half(out_acc[kk] / (warp_sum + 1e-6f));
        }
    }
}

torch::Tensor optimized_window_attention(
    torch::Tensor input, torch::Tensor rel_pos_table, 
    torch::Tensor qkv_weight, float logit_scale) {
    
    auto output = torch::empty({input.size(0), input.size(1), input.size(2), HEAD_DIM}, 
                              input.options().dtype(torch::kFloat16));
    
    const int batch_size = input.size(0);
    const int num_heads = qkv_weight.size(0);
    const int seq_len = WINDOW_SIZE * WINDOW_SIZE;
    const int embed_dim = input.size(2);

    const dim3 grid(seq_len, num_heads, batch_size);
    const size_t smem_size = (3 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + seq_len * seq_len) * sizeof(at::Half) + 
                            2 * seq_len * sizeof(float);

    optimized_window_attention_kernel<<<grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, smem_size>>>(
        input.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        rel_pos_table.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        qkv_weight.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        output.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        logit_scale,
        batch_size, num_heads, seq_len, embed_dim);
        
    return output;
}
"""

window_attn_cpp_source = "torch::Tensor optimized_window_attention(torch::Tensor input, torch::Tensor rel_pos_table, torch::Tensor qkv_weight, float logit_scale);"

window_attn_module = load_inline(
    name='window_attn',
    cpp_sources=window_attn_cpp_source,
    cuda_sources=window_attn_cuda_source,
    functions=['optimized_window_attention'],
    extra_cuda_cflags=['-O3', '--expt-relaxed-constexpr', '-lcublas', '-Xptxas=-v'],
    verbose=False
)

class WindowAttentionOptimized(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim, window_size, num_heads, qkv_bias=True, attn_drop=0., proj_drop=0., pretrained_window_size=[0, 0]):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.window_size = window_size
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = dim // num_heads
        self.logit_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.log(10 * torch.ones((num_heads, 1, 1))))
        
        self.qkv_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(num_heads, 3 * dim, self.head_dim) * 0.02)
        if qkv_bias:
            self.q_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
            self.v_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
        else:
            self.q_bias = self.v_bias = None

        self.cpb_mlp = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(2, 512, bias=True),
            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
            nn.Linear(512, num_heads, bias=False)
        )
        
        relative_coords_h = torch.arange(-(window_size[0]-1), window_size[0], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_w = torch.arange(-(window_size[1]-1), window_size[1], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_table = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(relative_coords_h, relative_coords_w, indexing="ij")).permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous().unsqueeze(0)
        if pretrained_window_size[0] > 0:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (pretrained_window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (pretrained_window_size[1]-1)
        else:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (window_size[1]-1)
        relative_coords_table *= 8
        relative_coords_table = torch.sign(relative_coords_table) * torch.log2(torch.abs(relative_coords_table) + 1.0) / np.log2(8)
        self.register_buffer("relative_coords_table", relative_coords_table.half())
        
        coords_h = torch.arange(window_size[0])
        coords_w = torch.arange(window_size[1])
        coords = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(coords_h, coords_w, indexing="ij"))
        coords_flatten = torch.flatten(coords, 1)
        relative_coords = coords_flatten[:, :, None] - coords_flatten[:, None, :]
        relative_coords = relative_coords.permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous()
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] += window_size[0]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 1] += window_size[1]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] *= 2*window_size[1]-1
        relative_position_index = relative_coords.sum(-1)
        self.register_buffer("relative_position_index", relative_position_index)

        self.proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.proj_drop = nn.Dropout(proj_drop)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None):
        B, N, C = x.shape
        logit_scale = torch.clamp(self.logit_scale, max=torch.log(torch.tensor(1./0.01))).exp().mean()
        
        x = x.half()
        rel_pos_bias = 16 * torch.sigmoid(self.cpb_mlp(self.relative_coords_table.view(-1, 2).float())
                         ).view(-1, self.num_heads)[self.relative_position_index.view(-1)]
        rel_pos_bias = rel_pos_bias.view(N, N, -1).permute(2, 0, 1).contiguous().half()

        x = window_attn_module.optimized_window_attention(
            x.transpose(0, 1), 
            rel_pos_bias,
            self.qkv_weight.half(),
            logit_scale.item()
        ).transpose(0, 1).float()
        
        x = self.proj(x)
        x = self.proj_drop(x)
        return x

class ModelNew(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, img_size=224, patch_size=4, in_chans=3, num_classes=1000,
                 embed_dim=96, depths=[2, 2, 6, 2], num_heads=[3, 6, 12, 24],

                 use_checkpoint=False, pretrained_window_sizes=[0, 0, 0, 0], **kwargs):

        self.num_layers = len(depths)
        self.embed_dim = embed_dim
        self.patch_norm = patch_norm

        dpr = [x.item() for x in torch.linspace(0, drop_path_rate, sum(depths))]

        self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
        for i_layer in range(self.num_layers):
            layer = BasicLayer(
                dim=int(embed_dim * 2 ** i_layer),
                input_resolution=(self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[0] // (2 ** i_layer),
                                self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[1] // (2 ** i_layer)),
                depth=depths[i_layer],
                num_heads=num_heads[i_layer],
                window_size=window_size,
                mlp_ratio=mlp_ratio,
                qkv_bias=qkv_bias,
                drop=drop_rate, 
                attn_drop=attn_drop_rate,
                drop_path=dpr[sum(depths[:i_layer]):sum(depths[:i_layer + 1])],
                norm_layer=norm_layer,
                downsample=PatchMerging if (i_layer < self.num_layers - 1) else None,
                use_checkpoint=use_checkpoint,
                pretrained_window_size=pretrained_window_sizes[i_layer])
            self.layers.append(layer)

        self.norm = norm_layer(self.num_features)
        self.avgpool = nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(1)
        self.head = nn.Linear(self.num_features, num_classes) if num_classes > 0 else nn.Identity()

    def forward_features(self, x):
        x = self.patch_embed(x)
        x = self.pos_drop(x)
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x)
        x = self.norm(x)
        x = self.avgpool(x.transpose(1, 2))
        x = torch.flatten(x, 1)
        return x

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.forward_features(x)
        x = self.head(x)
        return x

KERNEL_CODE:
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.cpp_extension import load_inline
import numpy as np
import collections
from itertools import repeat

def _ntuple(n):
    def parse(x):
        if isinstance(x, collections.abc.Iterable) and not isinstance(x, str):
            return tuple(x)
        return tuple(repeat(x, n))
    return parse
to_2tuple = _ntuple(2)

window_attn_cuda_source = """
#include <torch/extension.h>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cuda_pipeline.h>
#include <cooperative_groups.h>

constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = 8;
constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = WARPS_PER_BLOCK * 32;
constexpr int HEAD_DIM = 32;
constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 64;
constexpr int SMEM_BANK_STRIDE = 8;

__global__ void swin_attention_fused_kernel(
    const half2* __restrict__ input,
    const half2* __restrict__ rel_pos_table,
    const half2* __restrict__ qkv_weight,
    half2* __restrict__ output,
    const float logit_scale,
    const int batch_size,
    const int num_heads,
    const int seq_len,
    const int embed_dim) {
    
    using namespace cooperative_groups;
    thread_block tb = this_thread_block();
    thread_block_tile<32> warp = tiled_partition<32>(tb);
    
    extern __shared__ __align__(32) half smem[];
    half* q_smem = smem;
    half* k_smem = smem + seq_len * HEAD_DIM;
    half* v_smem = smem + 2 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM;
    float* attn_smem = (float*)(smem + 3 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM);
    
    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const int warp_id = tid / 32;
    const int lane_id = tid % 32;
    const int batch_head = blockIdx.z * num_heads + blockIdx.y;
    const int embed_dim_h2 = embed_dim / 2;
    
    // Fused QKV projection with vectorized loads
    const half2* x_ptr = input + batch_head * embed_dim_h2 * seq_len;
    const half2* weight_ptr = qkv_weight + blockIdx.y * 3 * embed_dim_h2 * HEAD_DIM;
    
    #pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        for (int j = warp_id; j < HEAD_DIM; j += WARPS_PER_BLOCK) {
            half2 acc = __float2half2_rn(0.0f);
            for (int k = lane_id; k < embed_dim_h2; k += 32) {
                half2 x_val = x_ptr[k * seq_len + blockIdx.x];
                half2 w_val = weight_ptr[i * embed_dim_h2 * HEAD_DIM + k * HEAD_DIM + j];
                acc = __hfma2(x_val, w_val, acc);
            }
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 16);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 8);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 4);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 2);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 1);
            
            if (lane_id == 0) {
                half* target = (i == 0) ? &q_smem[blockIdx.x * HEAD_DIM + j] :
                               (i == 1) ? &k_smem[blockIdx.x * HEAD_DIM + j] :
                                          &v_smem[blockIdx.x * HEAD_DIM + j];
                *target = __low2half(acc) + __high2half(acc);
            }
        }
    }
    tb.sync();

    // TensorCore attention computation
    const int num_tiles = (seq_len + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
    const int row = warp_id * 2;
    float max_val = -INFINITY;
    float sum_val = 0.0f;
    half2 acc[2][HEAD_DIM/32] = {};

    for (int tile = 0; tile < num_tiles; ++tile) {
        const int col_start = tile * TILE_SIZE;
        const int col_end = min(col_start + TILE_SIZE, seq_len);
        
        // Load K tile into shared memory
        for (int i = warp_id; i < TILE_SIZE; i += WARPS_PER_BLOCK) {
            int col = col_start + i;
            if (col < seq_len) {
                for (int j = lane_id; j < HEAD_DIM; j += 32) {
                    k_smem[i * HEAD_DIM + j] = k_smem[col * HEAD_DIM + j];
                }
            }
        }
        tb.sync();

        // Compute attention scores for tile
        for (int i = row; i < row + 2 && i < seq_len; ++i) {
            float scores[TILE_SIZE] = {0};
            for (int j = 0; j < HEAD_DIM; j += 16) {
                half q_val = q_smem[i * HEAD_DIM + j + lane_id % 16];
                #pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
                    half k_val = k_smem[(col_start + k) * HEAD_DIM + j + (lane_id + k) % 16];
                    scores[k] += __half2float(__hmul(q_val, k_val));
                }
            }
            
            // Apply logit scale and relative position bias
            #pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                int pos = i * seq_len + col_start + k;
                scores[k] = scores[k] * logit_scale + __half2float(rel_pos_table[pos]);
                max_val = fmaxf(max_val, scores[k]);
            }
            
            // Online softmax computation
            float sum_exp = 0.0f;
            #pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                sum_exp += expf(scores[k] - max_val);
            }
            sum_val = sum_val * expf(max_val - new_max) + sum_exp;
            max_val = new_max;

            // Accumulate attention values
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                float scale = expf(scores[k] - max_val) / (sum_val + 1e-6f);
                for (int v = 0; v < HEAD_DIM/32; ++v) {
                    half2 v_val = ((half2*)v_smem)[(col_start + k) * HEAD_DIM/32 + v];
                    acc[i-row][v] = __hfma2(__float2half2_rn(scale), v_val, acc[i-row][v]);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Store output with vectorized writes
    for (int i = row; i < row + 2 && i < seq_len; ++i) {
        half2* out_ptr = (half2*)(output + batch_head * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + i * HEAD_DIM);
        for (int v = 0; v < HEAD_DIM/32; ++v) {
            out_ptr[v * 16 + lane_id] = acc[i-row][v];
        }
    }
}

torch::Tensor swin_attention_fused(
    torch::Tensor input, torch::Tensor rel_pos_table,
    torch::Tensor qkv_weight, float logit_scale) {
    
    auto output = torch::empty_like(input);
    const int seq_len = rel_pos_table.size(0);
    const int batch_size = input.size(0);
    const int num_heads = qkv_weight.size(0);
    const int embed_dim = input.size(2) * 2;

    dim3 grid(seq_len, num_heads, batch_size);
    size_t smem_size = (3 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + seq_len * TILE_SIZE) * sizeof(half) +
                       seq_len * sizeof(float);

    swin_attention_fused_kernel<<<grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, smem_size>>>(
        (half2*)input.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        (half2*)rel_pos_table.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        (half2*)qkv_weight.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        (half2*)output.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        logit_scale,
        batch_size, num_heads, seq_len, embed_dim);
        
    return output;
}
"""

window_attn_cpp_source = "torch::Tensor swin_attention_fused(torch::Tensor input, torch::Tensor rel_pos_table, torch::Tensor qkv_weight, float logit_scale);"

window_attn_module = load_inline(
    name='window_attn',
    cpp_sources=window_attn_cpp_source,
    cuda_sources=window_attn_cuda_source,
    functions=['swin_attention_fused'],
    extra_cuda_cflags=['-O3', '--use_fast_math', '-lcublas', '-Xptxas=-v,-abi=no'],
    verbose=False
)

class WindowAttentionOptimized(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim, window_size, num_heads, qkv_bias=True, attn_drop=0., proj_drop=0., pretrained_window_size=[0, 0]):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.window_size = window_size
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = dim // num_heads
        self.logit_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.log(10 * torch.ones((num_heads, 1, 1))))
        
        self.qkv_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(num_heads, 3, dim, self.head_dim, device='cuda') * 0.02)
        if qkv_bias:
            self.q_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
            self.v_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
        else:
            self.q_bias = self.v_bias = None

        self.cpb_mlp = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(2, 512, bias=True),
            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
            nn.Linear(512, num_heads, bias=False)
        )
        
        relative_coords_h = torch.arange(-(window_size[0]-1), window_size[0], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_w = torch.arange(-(window_size[1]-1), window_size[1], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_table = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(relative_coords_h, relative_coords_w, indexing="ij")).permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous().unsqueeze(0)
        if pretrained_window_size[0] > 0:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (pretrained_window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (pretrained_window_size[1]-1)
        else:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (window_size[1]-1)
        relative_coords_table *= 8
        relative_coords_table = torch.sign(relative_coords_table) * torch.log2(torch.abs(relative_coords_table) + 1.0) / np.log2(8)
        self.register_buffer("relative_coords_table", relative_coords_table.half())
        
        coords_h = torch.arange(window_size[0])
        coords_w = torch.arange(window_size[1])
        coords = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(coords_h, coords_w, indexing="ij"))
        coords_flatten = torch.flatten(coords, 1)
        relative_coords = coords_flatten[:, :, None] - coords_flatten[:, None, :]
        relative_coords = relative_coords.permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous()
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] += window_size[0]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 1] += window_size[1]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] *= 2*window_size[1]-1
        relative_position_index = relative_coords.sum(-1)
        self.register_buffer("relative_position_index", relative_position_index)

        self.proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.proj_drop = nn.Dropout(proj_drop)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None):
        B, N, C = x.shape
        logit_scale = torch.clamp(self.logit_scale, max=torch.log(torch.tensor(1./0.01))).exp().mean()
        
        rel_pos_bias = 16 * torch.sigmoid(self.cpb_mlp(self.relative_coords_table.view(-1, 2).float())
                         ).view(-1, self.num_heads)[self.relative_position_index.view(-1)]
        rel_pos_bias = rel_pos_bias.view(N, N, -1).permute(2, 0, 1).contiguous().half()

        x = x.transpose(0, 1).half()
        qkv_weight = self.qkv_weight.view(self.num_heads, 3, -1).permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
        
        x = window_attn_module.swin_attention_fused(
            x, 
            rel_pos_bias,
            qkv_weight,
            logit_scale.item()
        ).float().transpose(0, 1)
        
        x = self.proj(x)
        x = self.proj_drop(x)
        return x

class ModelNew(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, img_size=224, patch_size=4, in_chans=3, num_classes=1000,
                 embed_dim=96, depths=[2, 2, 6, 2], num_heads=[3, 6, 12, 24],
                 window_size=7, mlp_ratio=4., qkv_bias=True,
                 drop_rate=0., attn_drop_rate=0., drop_path_rate=0.1,
                 norm_layer=nn.LayerNorm, patch_norm=True,
                 use_checkpoint=False, pretrained_window_sizes=[0, 0, 0, 0], **kwargs):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_classes = num_classes
        self.num_layers = len(depths)
        self.embed_dim = embed_dim
        self.patch_norm = patch_norm
        self.num_features = int(embed_dim * 2 ** (self.num_layers - 1))
        self.mlp_ratio = mlp_ratio

        self.patch_embed = PatchEmbed(
            img_size=img_size, patch_size=patch_size, in_chans=in_chans, embed_dim=embed_dim,
            norm_layer=norm_layer if self.patch_norm else None)
        self.pos_drop = nn.Dropout(p=drop_rate)
        dpr = [x.item() for x in torch.linspace(0, drop_path_rate, sum(depths))]

        self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
        for i_layer in range(self.num_layers):
            layer = BasicLayer(
                dim=int(embed_dim * 2 ** i_layer),
                input_resolution=(self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[0] // (2 ** i_layer),
                                self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[1] // (2 ** i_layer)),
                depth=depths[i_layer],
                num_heads=num_heads[i_layer],
                window_size=window_size,
                mlp_ratio=mlp_ratio,
                qkv_bias=qkv_bias,
                drop=drop_rate, 
                attn_drop=attn_drop_rate,
                drop_path=dpr[sum(depths[:i_layer]):sum(depths[:i_layer + 1])],
                norm_layer=norm_layer,
                downsample=PatchMerging if (i_layer < self.num_layers - 1) else None,
                use_checkpoint=use_checkpoint,
                pretrained_window_size=pretrained_window_sizes[i_layer])
            self.layers.append(layer)

        self.norm = norm_layer(self.num_features)
        self.avgpool = nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(1)
        self.head = nn.Linear(self.num_features, num_classes) if num_classes > 0 else nn.Identity()

    def forward_features(self, x):
        x = self.patch_embed(x)
        x = self.pos_drop(x)
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x)
        x = self.norm(x)
        x = self.avgpool(x.transpose(1, 2))
        x = torch.flatten(x, 1)
        return x

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.forward_features(x)
        x = self.head(x)
        return x

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.cpp_extension import load_inline
import numpy as np
import collections
from itertools import repeat

def _ntuple(n):
    def parse(x):
        if isinstance(x, collections.abc.Iterable) and not isinstance(x, str):
            return tuple(x)
        return tuple(repeat(x, n))
    return parse
to_2tuple = _ntuple(2)

window_attn_cuda_source = """
#include <torch/extension.h>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cuda_pipeline.h>
#include <cooperative_groups.h>

constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = 8;
constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = WARPS_PER_BLOCK * 32;
constexpr int HEAD_DIM = 32;
constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 64;
constexpr int SMEM_BANK_STRIDE = 8;

__global__ void swin_attention_fused_kernel(
    const half2* __restrict__ input,
    const half* __restrict__ rel_pos_table,
    const half2* __restrict__ qkv_weight,
    half2* __restrict__ output,
    const float logit_scale,
    const int batch_size,
    const int num_heads,
    const int seq_len,
    const int embed_dim) {
    
    using namespace cooperative_groups;
    thread_block tb = this_thread_block();
    thread_block_tile<32> warp = tiled_partition<32>(tb);
    
    extern __shared__ __align__(32) half smem[];
    half* q_smem = smem;
    half* k_smem = smem + seq_len * HEAD_DIM;
    half* v_smem = smem + 2 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM;
    float* attn_smem = (float*)(smem + 3 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM);
    
    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const int warp_id = tid / 32;
    const int lane_id = tid % 32;
    const int batch_head = blockIdx.z * num_heads + blockIdx.y;
    const int embed_dim_h2 = embed_dim / 2;
    
    // Fused QKV projection with vectorized loads
    const half2* x_ptr = input + batch_head * embed_dim_h2 * seq_len;
    const half2* weight_ptr = qkv_weight + blockIdx.y * 3 * embed_dim_h2 * HEAD_DIM;
    
    #pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        for (int j = warp_id; j < HEAD_DIM; j += WARPS_PER_BLOCK) {
            half2 acc = __float2half2_rn(0.0f);
            for (int k = lane_id; k < embed_dim_h2; k += 32) {
                half2 x_val = x_ptr[k * seq_len + blockIdx.x];
                half2 w_val = weight_ptr[i * embed_dim_h2 * HEAD_DIM + k * HEAD_DIM + j];
                acc = __hfma2(x_val, w_val, acc);
            }
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 16);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 8);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 4);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 2);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 1);
            
            if (lane_id == 0) {
                half* target = (i == 0) ? &q_smem[blockIdx.x * HEAD_DIM + j] :
                               (i == 1) ? &k_smem[blockIdx.x * HEAD_DIM + j] :
                                          &v_smem[blockIdx.x * HEAD_DIM + j];
                *target = __hadd(__low2half(acc), __high2half(acc));
            }
        }
    }
    tb.sync();

    // TensorCore attention computation
    const int num_tiles = (seq_len + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
    const int row = warp_id * 2;
    float max_val = -INFINITY;
    float sum_val = 0.0f;
    half2 acc[2][HEAD_DIM/32] = {};

    for (int tile = 0; tile < num_tiles; ++tile) {
        const int col_start = tile * TILE_SIZE;
        const int col_end = min(col_start + TILE_SIZE, seq_len);
        
        // Load K tile into shared memory
        for (int i = warp_id; i < TILE_SIZE; i += WARPS_PER_BLOCK) {
            int col = col_start + i;
            if (col < seq_len) {
                for (int j = lane_id; j < HEAD_DIM; j += 32) {
                    k_smem[i * HEAD_DIM + j] = k_smem[col * HEAD_DIM + j];
                }
            }
        }
        tb.sync();

        // Compute attention scores for tile
        for (int i = row; i < row + 2 && i < seq_len; ++i) {
            float scores[TILE_SIZE] = {0};
            for (int j = 0; j < HEAD_DIM; j += 16) {
                half q_val = q_smem[i * HEAD_DIM + j + lane_id % 16];
                #pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
                    half k_val = k_smem[(col_start + k) * HEAD_DIM + j + (lane_id + k) % 16];
                    scores[k] += __half2float(__hmul(q_val, k_val));
                }
            }
            
            // Apply logit scale and relative position bias
            #pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                int pos = i * seq_len + col_start + k;
                scores[k] = scores[k] * logit_scale + __half2float(rel_pos_table[pos]);
            }
            
            // Online softmax computation
            float tile_max = -INFINITY;
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                tile_max = fmaxf(tile_max, scores[k]);
            }
            float new_max = fmaxf(max_val, tile_max);
            
            float sum_exp = 0.0f;
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                sum_exp += expf(scores[k] - new_max);
            }
            
            sum_val = sum_val * expf(max_val - new_max) + sum_exp;
            max_val = new_max;

            // Accumulate attention values
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                float scale = expf(scores[k] - max_val) / (sum_val + 1e-6f);
                for (int v = 0; v < HEAD_DIM/32; ++v) {
                    half2 v_val = ((half2*)v_smem)[(col_start + k) * HEAD_DIM/32 + v];
                    acc[i-row][v] = __hfma2(__float2half2_rn(scale), v_val, acc[i-row][v]);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Store output with vectorized writes
    for (int i = row; i < row + 2 && i < seq_len; ++i) {
        half2* out_ptr = (half2*)(output + batch_head * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + i * HEAD_DIM);
        for (int v = 0; v < HEAD_DIM/32; ++v) {
            out_ptr[v * 16 + lane_id] = acc[i-row][v];
        }
    }
}

torch::Tensor swin_attention_fused(
    torch::Tensor input, torch::Tensor rel_pos_table,
    torch::Tensor qkv_weight, float logit_scale) {
    
    auto output = torch::empty_like(input);
    const int seq_len = rel_pos_table.size(0);
    const int batch_size = input.size(0);
    const int num_heads = qkv_weight.size(0);
    const int embed_dim = input.size(2) * 2;

    dim3 grid(seq_len, num_heads, batch_size);
    size_t smem_size = (3 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + seq_len * TILE_SIZE) * sizeof(half) +
                       seq_len * sizeof(float);

    swin_attention_fused_kernel<<<grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, smem_size>>>(
        (half2*)input.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        (half*)rel_pos_table.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        (half2*)qkv_weight.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        (half2*)output.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        logit_scale,
        batch_size, num_heads, seq_len, embed_dim);
        
    return output;
}
"""

window_attn_cpp_source = "torch::Tensor swin_attention_fused(torch::Tensor input, torch::Tensor rel_pos_table, torch::Tensor qkv_weight, float logit_scale);"

window_attn_module = load_inline(
    name='window_attn',
    cpp_sources=window_attn_cpp_source,
    cuda_sources=window_attn_cuda_source,
    functions=['swin_attention_fused'],
    extra_cuda_cflags=['-O3', '--use_fast_math', '-lcublas', '-Xptxas=-v,-abi=no'],
    verbose=False
)

class WindowAttentionOptimized(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim, window_size, num_heads, qkv_bias=True, attn_drop=0., proj_drop=0., pretrained_window_size=[0, 0]):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.window_size = window_size
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = dim // num_heads
        self.logit_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.log(10 * torch.ones((num_heads, 1, 1))))
        
        self.qkv_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(num_heads, 3, dim, self.head_dim, device='cuda') * 0.02)
        if qkv_bias:
            self.q_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
            self.v_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
        else:
            self.q_bias = self.v_bias = None

        self.cpb_mlp = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(2, 512, bias=True),
            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
            nn.Linear(512, num_heads, bias=False)
        )
        
        relative_coords_h = torch.arange(-(window_size[0]-1), window_size[0], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_w = torch.arange(-(window_size[1]-1), window_size[1], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_table = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(relative_coords_h, relative_coords_w, indexing="ij")).permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous().unsqueeze(0)
        if pretrained_window_size[0] > 0:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (pretrained_window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (pretrained_window_size[1]-1)
        else:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (window_size[1]-1)
        relative_coords_table *= 8
        relative_coords_table = torch.sign(relative_coords_table) * torch.log2(torch.abs(relative_coords_table) + 1.0) / np.log2(8)
        self.register_buffer("relative_coords_table", relative_coords_table.half())
        
        coords_h = torch.arange(window_size[0])
        coords_w = torch.arange(window_size[1])
        coords = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(coords_h, coords_w, indexing="ij"))
        coords_flatten = torch.flatten(coords, 1)
        relative_coords = coords_flatten[:, :, None] - coords_flatten[:, None, :]
        relative_coords = relative_coords.permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous()
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] += window_size[0]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 1] += window_size[1]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] *= 2*window_size[1]-1
        relative_position_index = relative_coords.sum(-1)
        self.register_buffer("relative_position_index", relative_position_index)

        self.proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.proj_drop = nn.Dropout(proj_drop)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None):
        B, N, C = x.shape
        logit_scale = torch.clamp(self.logit_scale, max=torch.log(torch.tensor(1./0.01))).exp().mean()
        
        rel_pos_bias = 16 * torch.sigmoid(self.cpb_mlp(self.relative_coords_table.view(-1, 2).float())
                         ).view(-1, self.num_heads)[self.relative_position_index.view(-1)]
        rel_pos_bias = rel_pos_bias.view(N, N, -1).permute(2, 0, 1).contiguous().half()

        x = x.transpose(0, 1).half()
        qkv_weight = self.qkv_weight.view(self.num_heads, 3, -1).permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
        
        x = window_attn_module.swin_attention_fused(
            x, 
            rel_pos_bias,
            qkv_weight,
            logit_scale.item()
        ).float().transpose(0, 1)
        
        x = self.proj(x)
        x = self.proj_drop(x)
        return x

class ModelNew(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, img_size=224, patch_size=4, in_chans=3, num_classes=1000,
                 embed_dim=96, depths=[2, 2, 6, 2], num_heads=[3, 6, 12, 24],
                 window_size=7, mlp_ratio=4., qkv_bias=True,
                 drop_rate=0., attn_drop_rate=0., drop_path_rate=0.1,
                 norm_layer=nn.LayerNorm, patch_norm=True,
                 use_checkpoint=False, pretrained_window_sizes=[0, 0, 0, 0], **kwargs):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_classes = num_classes
        self.num_layers = len(depths)
        self.embed_dim = embed_dim
        self.patch_norm = patch_norm
        self.num_features = int(embed_dim * 2 ** (self.num_layers - 1))
        self.mlp_ratio = mlp_ratio

        self.patch_embed = PatchEmbed(
            img_size=img_size, patch_size=patch_size, in_chans=in_chans, embed_dim=embed_dim,
            norm_layer=norm_layer if self.patch_norm else None)
        self.pos_drop = nn.Dropout(p=drop_rate)
        dpr = [x.item() for x in torch.linspace(0, drop_path_rate, sum(depths))]

        self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
        for i_layer in range(self.num_layers):
            layer = BasicLayer(
                dim=int(embed_dim * 2 ** i_layer),
                input_resolution=(self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[0] // (2 ** i_layer),
                                self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[1] // (2 ** i_layer)),
                depth=depths[i_layer],
                num_heads=num_heads[i_layer],
                window_size=window_size,
                mlp_ratio=mlp_ratio,
                qkv_bias=qkv_bias,
                drop=drop_rate, 
                attn_drop=attn_drop_rate,
                drop_path=dpr[sum(depths[:i_layer]):sum(depths[:i_layer + 1])],
                norm_layer=norm_layer,
                downsample=PatchMerging if (i_layer < self.num_layers - 1) else None,
                use_checkpoint=use_checkpoint,
                pretrained_window_size=pretrained_window_sizes[i_layer])
            self.layers.append(layer)

        self.norm = norm_layer(self.num_features)
        self.avgpool = nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(1)
        self.head = nn.Linear(self.num_features, num_classes) if num_classes > 0 else nn.Identity()

    def forward_features(self, x):
        x = self.patch_embed(x)
        x = self.pos_drop(x)
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x)
        x = self.norm(x)
        x = self.avgpool(x.transpose(1, 2))
        x = torch.flatten(x, 1)
        return x

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.forward_features(x)
        x = self.head(x)
        return x

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.cpp_extension import load_inline
import numpy as np
import collections
from itertools import repeat

def _ntuple(n):
    def parse(x):
        if isinstance(x, collections.abc.Iterable) and not isinstance(x, str):
            return tuple(x)
        return tuple(repeat(x, n))
    return parse
to_2tuple = _ntuple(2)

window_attn_cuda_source = """
#include <torch/extension.h>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cuda_pipeline.h>
#include <cooperative_groups.h>

constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = 8;
constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = WARPS_PER_BLOCK * 32;
constexpr int HEAD_DIM = 32;
constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 64;

__global__ void swin_attention_fused_kernel(
    const half2* __restrict__ input,
    const half* __restrict__ rel_pos_table,
    const half2* __restrict__ qkv_weight,
    half2* __restrict__ output,
    const float logit_scale,
    const int batch_size,
    const int num_heads,
    const int seq_len,
    const int embed_dim) {
    
    using namespace cooperative_groups;
    thread_block tb = this_thread_block();
    thread_block_tile<32> warp = tiled_partition<32>(tb);
    
    extern __shared__ __align__(32) half smem[];
    half* q_smem = smem;
    half* k_smem = smem + seq_len * HEAD_DIM;
    half* v_smem = smem + 2 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM;
    
    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const int warp_id = tid / 32;
    const int lane_id = tid % 32;
    const int batch_head = blockIdx.z * num_heads + blockIdx.y;
    const int embed_dim_h2 = embed_dim / 2;
    
    // Fused QKV projection with vectorized loads
    const half2* x_ptr = input + batch_head * embed_dim_h2 * seq_len;
    const half2* weight_ptr = qkv_weight + blockIdx.y * 3 * embed_dim_h2 * HEAD_DIM;
    
    #pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        for (int j = warp_id; j < HEAD_DIM; j += WARPS_PER_BLOCK) {
            half2 acc = __float2half2_rn(0.0f);
            for (int k = lane_id; k < embed_dim_h2; k += 32) {
                half2 x_val = x_ptr[k * seq_len + blockIdx.x];
                half2 w_val = weight_ptr[i * embed_dim_h2 * HEAD_DIM + k * HEAD_DIM + j];
                acc = __hfma2(x_val, w_val, acc);
            }
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 16);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 8);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 4);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 2);
            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 1);
            
            if (lane_id == 0) {
                half* target = (i == 0) ? &q_smem[blockIdx.x * HEAD_DIM + j] :
                               (i == 1) ? &k_smem[blockIdx.x * HEAD_DIM + j] :
                                          &v_smem[blockIdx.x * HEAD_DIM + j];
                *target = __hadd(__low2half(acc), __high2half(acc));
            }
        }
    }
    tb.sync();

    // TensorCore attention computation
    const int num_tiles = (seq_len + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
    const int row = warp_id * 2;
    float max_val = -INFINITY;
    float sum_val = 0.0f;
    half2 acc[2][HEAD_DIM/32] = {};

    for (int tile = 0; tile < num_tiles; ++tile) {
        const int col_start = tile * TILE_SIZE;
        const int col_end = min(col_start + TILE_SIZE, seq_len);
        
        // Load K tile into shared memory
        for (int i = warp_id; i < TILE_SIZE; i += WARPS_PER_BLOCK) {
            int col = col_start + i;
            if (col < seq_len) {
                for (int j = lane_id; j < HEAD_DIM; j += 32) {
                    k_smem[i * HEAD_DIM + j] = k_smem[col * HEAD_DIM + j];
                }
            }
        }
        tb.sync();

        // Compute attention scores for tile
        for (int i = row; i < row + 2 && i < seq_len; ++i) {
            float scores[TILE_SIZE] = {0};
            for (int j = 0; j < HEAD_DIM; j += 16) {
                half q_val = q_smem[i * HEAD_DIM + j + lane_id % 16];
                #pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
                    half k_val = k_smem[(col_start + k) * HEAD_DIM + j + (lane_id + k) % 16];
                    scores[k] += __half2float(__hmul(q_val, k_val));
                }
            }
            
            // Apply logit scale and relative position bias
            #pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                int pos = i * seq_len + col_start + k;
                scores[k] = scores[k] * logit_scale + __half2float(rel_pos_table[pos]);
            }
            
            // Online softmax computation
            float tile_max = -INFINITY;
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                tile_max = fmaxf(tile_max, scores[k]);
            }
            float new_max = fmaxf(max_val, tile_max);
            
            float sum_exp = 0.0f;
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                sum_exp += expf(scores[k] - new_max);
            }
            
            sum_val = sum_val * expf(max_val - new_max) + sum_exp;
            max_val = new_max;

            // Accumulate attention values
            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
                float scale = expf(scores[k] - max_val) / (sum_val + 1e-6f);
                for (int v = 0; v < HEAD_DIM/32; ++v) {
                    half2 v_val = ((half2*)v_smem)[(col_start + k) * HEAD_DIM/32 + v];
                    acc[i-row][v] = __hfma2(__float2half2_rn(scale), v_val, acc[i-row][v]);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Store output with vectorized writes
    for (int i = row; i < row + 2 && i < seq_len; ++i) {
        half2* out_ptr = (half2*)(output + batch_head * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + i * HEAD_DIM);
        for (int v = 0; v < HEAD_DIM/32; ++v) {
            out_ptr[v * 16 + lane_id] = acc[i-row][v];
        }
    }
}

torch::Tensor swin_attention_fused(
    torch::Tensor input, torch::Tensor rel_pos_table,
    torch::Tensor qkv_weight, float logit_scale) {
    
    auto output = torch::empty_like(input);
    const int seq_len = rel_pos_table.size(0);
    const int batch_size = input.size(0);
    const int num_heads = qkv_weight.size(0);
    const int embed_dim = input.size(2) * 2;

    dim3 grid(seq_len, num_heads, batch_size);
    size_t smem_size = (3 * seq_len * HEAD_DIM) * sizeof(half);

    swin_attention_fused_kernel<<<grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, smem_size>>>(
        (half2*)input.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        (half*)rel_pos_table.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        (half2*)qkv_weight.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        (half2*)output.data_ptr<at::Half>(),
        logit_scale,
        batch_size, num_heads, seq_len, embed_dim);
        
    return output;
}
"""

window_attn_cpp_source = "torch::Tensor swin_attention_fused(torch::Tensor input, torch::Tensor rel_pos_table, torch::Tensor qkv_weight, float logit_scale);"

window_attn_module = load_inline(
    name='window_attn',
    cpp_sources=window_attn_cpp_source,
    cuda_sources=window_attn_cuda_source,
    functions=['swin_attention_fused'],
    extra_cuda_cflags=['-O3', '--use_fast_math', '-lcublas', '-Xptxas=-v,-abi=no'],
    verbose=False
)

class WindowAttentionOptimized(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim, window_size, num_heads, qkv_bias=True, attn_drop=0., proj_drop=0., pretrained_window_size=[0, 0]):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.window_size = window_size
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = dim // num_heads
        self.logit_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.log(10 * torch.ones((num_heads, 1, 1))))
        
        self.qkv_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(num_heads, 3, dim, self.head_dim, device='cuda') * 0.02)
        if qkv_bias:
            self.q_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
            self.v_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))
        else:
            self.q_bias = self.v_bias = None

        self.cpb_mlp = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(2, 512, bias=True),
            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
            nn.Linear(512, num_heads, bias=False)
        )
        
        relative_coords_h = torch.arange(-(window_size[0]-1), window_size[0], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_w = torch.arange(-(window_size[1]-1), window_size[1], dtype=torch.float32)
        relative_coords_table = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(relative_coords_h, relative_coords_w, indexing="ij")).permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous().unsqueeze(0)
        if pretrained_window_size[0] > 0:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (pretrained_window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (pretrained_window_size[1]-1)
        else:
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (window_size[0]-1)
            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (window_size[1]-1)
        relative_coords_table *= 8
        relative_coords_table = torch.sign(relative_coords_table) * torch.log2(torch.abs(relative_coords_table) + 1.0) / np.log2(8)
        self.register_buffer("relative_coords_table", relative_coords_table.half())
        
        coords_h = torch.arange(window_size[0])
        coords_w = torch.arange(window_size[1])
        coords = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid(coords_h, coords_w, indexing="ij"))
        coords_flatten = torch.flatten(coords, 1)
        relative_coords = coords_flatten[:, :, None] - coords_flatten[:, None, :]
        relative_coords = relative_coords.permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous()
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] += window_size[0]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 1] += window_size[1]-1
        relative_coords[:, :, 0] *= 2*window_size[1]-1
        relative_position_index = relative_coords.sum(-1)
        self.register_buffer("relative_position_index", relative_position_index)

        self.proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.proj_drop = nn.Dropout(proj_drop)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None):
        B, N, C = x.shape
        logit_scale = torch.clamp(self.logit_scale, max=torch.log(torch.tensor(1./0.01))).exp().mean()
        
        rel_pos_bias = 16 * torch.sigmoid(self.cpb_mlp(self.relative_coords_table.view(-1, 2).float())
                         ).view(-1, self.num_heads)[self.relative_position_index.view(-1)]
        rel_pos_bias = rel_pos_bias.view(N, N, -1).permute(2, 0, 1).contiguous().half()

        x = x.transpose(0, 1).half()
        qkv_weight = self.qkv_weight.view(self.num_heads, 3, -1).permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
        
        x = window_attn_module.swin_attention_fused(
            x, 
            rel_pos_bias,
            qkv_weight,
            logit_scale.item()
        ).float().transpose(0, 1)
        
        x = self.proj(x)
        x = self.proj_drop(x)
        return x

class ModelNew(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, img_size=224, patch_size=4, in_chans=3, num_classes=1000,
                 embed_dim=96, depths=[2, 2, 6, 2], num_heads=[3, 6, 12, 24],
                 window_size=7, mlp_ratio=4., qkv_bias=True,
                 drop_rate=0., attn_drop_rate=0., drop_path_rate=0.1,
                 norm_layer=nn.LayerNorm, patch_norm=True,
                 use_checkpoint=False, pretrained_window_sizes=[0, 0, 0, 0], **kwargs):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_classes = num_classes
        self.num_layers = len(depths)
        self.embed_dim = embed_dim
        self.patch_norm = patch_norm
        self.num_features = int(embed_dim * 2 ** (self.num_layers - 1))
        self.mlp_ratio = mlp_ratio

        self.patch_embed = PatchEmbed(
            img_size=img_size, patch_size=patch_size, in_chans=in_chans, embed_dim=embed_dim,
            norm_layer=norm_layer if self.patch_norm else None)
        self.pos_drop = nn.Dropout(p=drop_rate)
        dpr = [x.item() for x in torch.linspace(0, drop_path_rate, sum(depths))]

        self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
        for i_layer in range(self.num_layers):
            layer = BasicLayer(
                dim=int(embed_dim * 2 ** i_layer),
                input_resolution=(self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[0] // (2 ** i_layer),
                                self.patch_embed.patches_resolution[1] // (2 ** i_layer)),
                depth=depths[i_layer],
                num_heads=num_heads[i_layer],
                window_size=window_size,
                mlp_ratio=mlp_ratio,
                qkv_bias=qkv_bias,
                drop=drop_rate, 
                attn_drop=attn_drop_rate,
                drop_path=dpr[sum(depths[:i_layer]):sum(depths[:i_layer + 1])],
                norm_layer=norm_layer,
                downsample=PatchMerging if (i_layer < self.num_layers - 1) else None,
                use_checkpoint=use_checkpoint,
                pretrained_window_size=pretrained_window_sizes[i_layer])
            self.layers.append(layer)

        self.norm = norm_layer(self.num_features)
        self.avgpool = nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(1)
        self.head = nn.Linear(self.num_features, num_classes) if num_classes > 0 else nn.Identity()

    def forward_features(self, x):
        x = self.patch_embed(x)
        x = self.pos_drop(x)
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x)
        x = self.norm(x)
        x = self.avgpool(x.transpose(1, 2))
        x = torch.flatten(x, 1)
        return x

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.forward_features(x)
        x = self.head(x)
        return x

WindowAttention(nn.Module):\n    r\"\"\" Window based multi-head self attention (W-MSA) module with relative position bias.\n    It supports both of shifted and non-shifted window.\n\n    Args:\n        dim (int): Number of input channels.\n        window_size (tuple[int]): The height and width of the window.\n        num_heads (int): Number of attention heads.\n        qkv_bias (bool, optional):  If True, add a learnable bias to query, key, value. Default: True\n        attn_drop (float, optional): Dropout ratio of attention weight. Default: 0.0\n        proj_drop (float, optional): Dropout ratio of output. Default: 0.0\n        pretrained_window_size (tuple[int]): The height and width of the window in pre-training.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, dim, window_size, num_heads, qkv_bias=True, attn_drop=0., proj_drop=0.,\n                 pretrained_window_size=[0, 0]):\n\n        super().__init__()\n        self.dim = dim\n        self.window_size = window_size  # Wh, Ww\n        self.pretrained_window_size = pretrained_window_size\n        self.num_heads = num_heads\n\n        self.logit_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.log(10 * torch.ones((num_heads, 1, 1))), requires_grad=True)\n\n        # mlp to generate continuous relative position bias\n        self.cpb_mlp = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(2, 512, bias=True),\n                                     nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n                                     nn.Linear(512, num_heads, bias=False))\n\n        # get relative_coords_table\n        relative_coords_h = torch.arange(-(self.window_size[0] - 1), self.window_size[0], dtype=torch.float32)\n        relative_coords_w = torch.arange(-(self.window_size[1] - 1), self.window_size[1], dtype=torch.float32)\n        relative_coords_table = torch.stack(\n            torch.meshgrid([relative_coords_h,\n                            relative_coords_w])).permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous().unsqueeze(0)  # 1, 2*Wh-1, 2*Ww-1, 2\n        if pretrained_window_size[0] > 0:\n            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (pretrained_window_size[0] - 1)\n            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (pretrained_window_size[1] - 1)\n        else:\n            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 0] /= (self.window_size[0] - 1)\n            relative_coords_table[:, :, :, 1] /= (self.window_size[1] - 1)\n        relative_coords_table *= 8  # normalize to -8, 8\n        relative_coords_table = torch.sign(relative_coords_table) * torch.log2(\n            torch.abs(relative_coords_table) + 1.0) / np.log2(8)\n\n        self.register_buffer(\"relative_coords_table\", relative_coords_table)\n\n        # get pair-wise relative position index for each token inside the window\n        coords_h = torch.arange(self.window_size[0])\n        coords_w = torch.arange(self.window_size[1])\n        coords = torch.stack(torch.meshgrid([coords_h, coords_w]))  # 2, Wh, Ww\n        coords_flatten = torch.flatten(coords, 1)  # 2, Wh*Ww\n        relative_coords = coords_flatten[:, :, None] - coords_flatten[:, None, :]  # 2, Wh*Ww, Wh*Ww\n        relative_coords = relative_coords.permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous()  # Wh*Ww, Wh*Ww, 2\n        relative_coords[:, :, 0] += self.window_size[0] - 1  # shift to start from 0\n        relative_coords[:, :, 1] += self.window_size[1] - 1\n        relative_coords[:, :, 0] *= 2 * self.window_size[1] - 1\n        relative_position_index = relative_coords.sum(-1)  # Wh*Ww, Wh*Ww\n        self.register_buffer(\"relative_position_index\", relative_position_index)\n\n        self.qkv = nn.Linear(dim, dim * 3, bias=False)\n        if qkv_bias:\n            self.q_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))\n            self.v_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))\n        else:\n            self.q_bias = None\n            self.v_bias = None\n        self.attn_drop = nn.Dropout(attn_drop)\n        self.proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)\n        self.proj_drop = nn.Dropout(proj_drop)\n        self.softmax = nn.Softmax(dim=-1)\n\n    def forward(self, x, mask=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Args:\n            x: input features with shape of (num_windows*B, N, C)\n            mask: (0/-inf) mask with shape of (num_windows, Wh*Ww, Wh*Ww) or None\n        \"\"\"\n        B_, N, C = x.shape\n        qkv_bias = None\n        if self.q_bias is not None:\n            qkv_bias = torch.cat((self.q_bias, torch.zeros_like(self.v_bias, requires_grad=False), self.v_bias))\n        qkv = F.linear(input=x, weight=self.qkv.weight, bias=qkv_bias)\n        qkv = qkv.reshape(B_, N, 3, self.num_heads, -1).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)\n        q, k, v = qkv[0], qkv[1], qkv[2]  # make torchscript happy (cannot use tensor as tuple)\n\n        # cosine attention\n        attn = (F.normalize(q, dim=-1) @ F.normalize(k, dim=-1).transpose(-2, -1))\n        logit_scale = torch.clamp(self.logit_scale.to(x.device), max=torch.log(torch.tensor(1. / 0.01, device=x.device))).exp()\n        attn = attn * logit_scale\n\n        relative_position_bias_table = self.cpb_mlp(self.relative_coords_table).view(-1, self.num_heads)\n        relative_position_bias = relative_position_bias_table[self.relative_position_index.view(-1)].view(\n            self.window_size[0] * self.window_size[1], self.window_size[0] * self.window_size[1], -1)  # Wh*Ww,Wh*Ww,nH\n        relative_position_bias = relative_position_bias.permute(2, 0, 1).contiguous()  # nH, Wh*Ww, Wh*Ww\n        relative_position_bias = 16 * torch.sigmoid(relative_position_bias)\n        attn = attn + relative_position_bias.unsqueeze(0)\n\n        if mask is not None:\n            nW = mask.shape[0]\n            attn = attn.view(B_ // nW, nW, self.num_heads, N, N) + mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(0)\n            attn = attn.view(-1, self.num_heads, N, N)\n            attn = self.softmax(attn)\n        else:\n            attn = self.softmax(attn)\n\n        attn = self.attn_drop(attn)\n\n        x = (attn @ v).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B_, N, C)\n        x = self.proj(x)\n        x = self.proj_drop(x)\n        return x\n\nclass SwinTransformerBlock(nn.Module):\n    r\"\"\" Swin Transformer Block.\n\n    Args:\n        dim (int): Number of input channels.\n        input_resolution (tuple[int]): Input resulotion.\n        num_heads (int): Number of attention heads.\n        window_size (int): Window size.\n        shift_size (int): Shift size for SW-MSA.\n        mlp_ratio (float): Ratio of mlp hidden dim to embedding dim.\n        qkv_bias (bool, optional): If True, add a learnable bias to query, key, value. Default: True\n        drop (float, optional): Dropout rate. Default: 0.0\n        attn_drop (float, optional): Attention dropout rate. Default: 0.0\n        drop_path (float, optional): Stochastic depth rate. Default: 0.0\n        act_layer (nn.Module, optional): Activation layer. Default: nn.GELU\n        norm_layer (nn.Module, optional): Normalization layer.  Default: nn.LayerNorm\n        pretrained_window_size (int): Window size in pre-training.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, dim, input_resolution, num_heads, window_size=7, shift_size=0,\n                 mlp_ratio=4., qkv_bias=True, drop=0., attn_drop=0., drop_path=0.,\n                 act_layer=nn.GELU, norm_layer=nn.LayerNorm, pretrained_window_size=0):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.dim = dim\n        self.input_resolution = input_resolution\n        self.num_heads = num_heads\n        self.window_size = window_size\n        self.shift_size = shift_size\n        self.mlp_ratio = mlp_ratio\n        if min(self.input_resolution) <= self.window_size:\n            # if window size is larger than input resolution, we don't partition windows\n            self.shift_size = 0\n            self.window_size = min(self.input_resolution)\n        assert 0 <= self.shift_size < self.window_size, \"shift_size must in 0-window_size\"\n\n        self.norm1 = norm_layer(dim)\n        self.attn = WindowAttention(\n            dim, window_size=to_2tuple(self.window_size), num_heads=num_heads,\n            qkv_bias=qkv_bias, attn_drop=attn_drop, proj_drop=drop,\n            pretrained_window_size=to_2tuple(pretrained_window_size))\n\n        self.drop_path = nn.Identity()\n        self.norm2 = norm_layer(dim)\n        mlp_hidden_dim = int(dim * mlp_ratio)\n        self.mlp = Mlp(in_features=dim, hidden_features=mlp_hidden_dim, act_layer=act_layer, drop=drop)\n\n        if self.shift_size > 0:\n            # calculate attention mask for SW-MSA\n            H, W = self.input_resolution\n            img_mask = torch.zeros((1, H, W, 1))  # 1 H W 1\n            h_slices = (slice(0, -self.window_size),\n                        slice(-self.window_size, -self.shift_size),\n                        slice(-self.shift_size, None))\n            w_slices = (slice(0, -self.window_size),\n                        slice(-self.window_size, -self.shift_size),\n                        slice(-self.shift_size, None))\n            cnt = 0\n            for h in h_slices:\n                for w in w_slices:\n                    img_mask[:, h, w, :] = cnt\n                    cnt += 1\n\n            mask_windows = window_partition(img_mask, self.window_size)  # nW, window_size, window_size, 1\n            mask_windows = mask_windows.view(-1, self.window_size * self.window_size)\n            attn_mask = mask_windows.unsqueeze(1) - mask_windows.unsqueeze(2)\n            attn_mask = attn_mask.masked_fill(attn_mask != 0, float(-100.0)).masked_fill(attn_mask == 0, float(0.0))\n        else:\n            attn_mask = None\n\n        self.register_buffer(\"attn_mask\", attn_mask)\n\n    def forward(self, x):\n        H, W = self.input_resolution\n        B, L, C = x.shape\n        assert L == H * W, \"input feature has wrong size\"\n\n        shortcut = x\n        x = x.view(B, H, W, C)\n\n        # cyclic shift\n        if self.shift_size > 0:\n            shifted_x = torch.roll(x, shifts=(-self.shift_size, -self.shift_size), dims=(1, 2))\n        else:\n            shifted_x = x\n\n        # partition windows\n        x_windows = window_partition(shifted_x, self.window_size)  # nW*B, window_size, window_size, C\n        x_windows = x_windows.view(-1, self.window_size * self.window_size, C)  # nW*B, window_size*window_size, C\n\n        # W-MSA/SW-MSA\n        attn_windows = self.attn(x_windows, mask=self.attn_mask)  # nW*B, window_size*window_size, C\n\n        # merge windows\n        attn_windows = attn_windows.view(-1, self.window_size, self.window_size, C)\n        shifted_x = window_reverse(attn_windows, self.window_size, H, W)  # B H' W' C\n\n        # reverse cyclic shift\n        if self.shift_size > 0:\n            x = torch.roll(shifted_x, shifts=(self.shift_size, self.shift_size), dims=(1, 2))\n        else:\n            x = shifted_x\n        x = x.view(B, H * W, C)\n        x = shortcut + self.drop_path(self.norm1(x))\n\n        # FFN\n        x = x + self.drop_path(self.norm2(self.mlp(x)))\n\n        return x\n\n\nclass PatchMerging(nn.Module):\n    r\"\"\" Patch Merging Layer.\n\n    Args:\n        input_resolution (tuple[int]): Resolution of input feature.\n        dim (int): Number of input channels.\n        norm_layer (nn.Module, optional): Normalization layer.  Default: nn.LayerNorm\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, input_resolution, dim, norm_layer=nn.LayerNorm):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.input_resolution = input_resolution\n        self.dim = dim\n        self.reduction = nn.Linear(4 * dim, 2 * dim, bias=False)\n        self.norm = norm_layer(2 * dim)\n\n    def forward(self, x):\n        \"\"\"\n        x: B, H*W, C\n        \"\"\"\n        H, W = self.input_resolution\n        B, L, C = x.shape\n        assert L == H * W, \"input feature has wrong size\"\n        assert H % 2 == 0 and W % 2 == 0, f\"x size ({H}*{W}) are not even.\"\n\n        x = x.view(B, H, W, C)\n\n        x0 = x[:, 0::2, 0::2, :]  # B H/2 W/2 C\n        x1 = x[:, 1::2, 0::2, :]  # B H/2 W/2 C\n        x2 = x[:, 0::2, 1::2, :]  # B H/2 W/2 C\n        x3 = x[:, 1::2, 1::2, :]  # B H/2 W/2 C\n        x = torch.cat([x0, x1, x2, x3], -1)  # B H/2 W/2 4*C\n        x = x.view(B, -1, 4 * C)  # B H/2*W/2 4*C\n\n        x = self.reduction(x)\n        x = self.norm(x)\n\n        return x\n\nclass BasicLayer(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\" A basic Swin Transformer layer for one stage.\n\n    Args:\n        dim (int): Number of input channels.\n        input_resolution (tuple[int]): Input resolution.\n        depth (int): Number of blocks.\n        num_heads (int): Number of attention heads.\n        window_size (int): Local window size.\n        mlp_ratio (float): Ratio of mlp hidden dim to embedding dim.\n        qkv_bias (bool, optional): If True, add a learnable bias to query, key, value. __hfma2(x_val, w_val, acc);\n            }\n            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 16);\n            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 8);\n            acc = warp.shfl_down(acc, 4);\n            acc = __half2float(__hmul(q_val, k_val));\n                }\n            }\n            \n            // Apply logit scale and relative position bias\n            #pragma unroll\n            for (int k =            // Online softmax computation\n            float tile_max = -INFINITY;\n            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {\n                tile_max = fmaxf(tile_max, scores[k]);\n - new_max);\n            }\n            \n            sum_val = sum_val * expf(max_val - new_max) + sum_exp;\n            max_val = new_max;\n\n            // Accumulate attention values\n            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {\n                float scale = expf(scores[k] - max_val) / (sum_val + 1e-6f);\n                for (int v = 0; v < HEAD_DIM/32; ++v) {\n                    half2 HEAD_DIM);\n        for (int v = 0; v < HEAD_DIM/32; ++v) {\n            out_ptr[v * 16 + lane_id] = acc[i-row][v];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ntorch::Tensor swin_attention_fused(\n    torch::Tensor logit_scale,\n        batch_size, num_heads, seq_len, embed_dim);\n        \n    return output;\n}\n\"\"\"\n\nwindow_attn_cpp_source = \"torch::Tensor swin_attention_fused(torch::Tensor input, WindowAttentionOptimized(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, dim, window_size, num_heads, qkv_bias=True, attn_drop=0., proj_drop=0., pretrained_window_size=[0, 0]):\n        super().__init__()\n 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {\n                tile_max = fmaxf(tile_max, scores[k]);\n            }\n            float new_max = fmaxf(max_val, tile_max);\n            \n            float sum_exp = 0.0f;\n            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {\n                sum_exp += expf(scores[k] - new_max);\n            }\n            \n            sum_val = sum_val * expf(max_val - new_max) + sum_exp;\n            max_val = new_max;\n\n            // Accumulate attention values\n            for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {\n                float scale = expf(scores[k] - max_val) / __hfma2(__float2half2_rn(scale), v_val, acc[i-row][v]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Store output with vectorized writes\n    for (int i = row; i < row + 2 && i < )\n        \n        relative_coords_h = torch.arange(-(window_size[0]-1), window_size[0], dtype=torch.float32)\n        relative_coords_w = torch.arange(-(window_size[1]-1), window_size[1],
